    SELECT `p1`.`ProductUnitId`, `p1`.`ConversionValue`, `p1`.`HargaJual`, `p1`.`HargaModal`, `p1`.`IdProduk`, `p1`.`IsDeleted`, `p1`.`Is_Default`, `p1`.`UnitName`, `p1`.`c`
    FROM (
        SELECT `p0`.`ProductUnitId`, `p0`.`ConversionValue`, `p0`.`HargaJual`, `p0`.`HargaModal`, `p0`.`IdProduk`, `p0`.`IsDeleted`, `p0`.`Is_Default`, `p0`.`UnitName`, 1 AS `c`, ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY `p0`.`IdProduk` ORDER BY `p0`.`ProductUnitId`) AS `row`
        FROM `ProductUnits` AS `p0`
        WHERE NOT (`p0`.`IsDeleted`)
    ) AS `p1`
    WHERE `p1`.`row` <= 1
) AS `p2` ON `p`.`IdProduk` = `p2`.`IdProduk`
